See experiment report and archived raw evidence.

- Added default-off dual input/block-0 LayerNorm. Corrected scalar-conversion mismatch using the incumbent helper path; real-weight 97-state intermediates and p1–10/p1000 score dumps match exactly. Final H200 memcheck/synccheck report zero errors.

## 2026-09-15 H200 compact57 optimization
- Added opt-in `BEAM_STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_COMPACT57=1` to use 57 physical token rows for Cube4. Scratch allocation, network dimensions and per-lane offsets share one layout selector; attention auxiliary scratch retains its existing 64-row safety bound. Default layouts are unchanged. Freeze the flag before allocation/capture.
- Shape regression demonstrated RED before implementation and passed after; CUDA final-CLS test passed, baseline/optimized score dumps matched for CSV-derived inputs p1-10 and p1000, and physical H200 memcheck reported zero errors.
- Combined existing Hopper FP16 TMA QKV/FF1 + FF2 m128n128 + compact57 measured 605,832.6 parents/s at m384/c8 (three processes x100 replays), versus 459,838.5 original repeated baseline. Full pipeline verification remains in progress; no low-precision arithmetic or new model was introduced.
- Added reproducible H200 sweeps and diagnostic summarizers. The synthetic input generator repeats states, so its exact-score gate is insufficient alone. Benchmark TFLOPS accounting still requires correction for generic final CLS-only.
- Completed 100M integrated depth7/8 at 179.381/179.310s versus original 231.305/231.321s, about +29% throughput. Matched-batch legacy depth5 reproduces optimized candidate count exactly. Optimized Nsight confirms 35 rather than 42 kernels/forward. Archived all evidence; stopped GPU after measurements while retaining billable disk.

## 2026-08-02
- Fixed Cube-444 native Stream1 inference parity by making the fused CUTLASS FF1 epilogue activation manifest-driven (`relu` for `cube4`, `silu` for `p900`) and by making the PyTorch parity oracle use the same manifest field. Unknown activation values fail closed. The MLP path is unchanged. Root cause was confirmed on A100 job 33339: LibTorch honored `activation=relu`, while native CUDA and the Python oracle had hardcoded SiLU. Evidence: `test_results/cube444_transformer_activation_parity_fix_2026-08-02.md`.

- Added `test_build_result_envelope_accepts_piece_transformer_format` to verify deterministic envelope building preserves the new `piece-transformer` format and avoids silent schema rejection of public Transformer runs.
- Prepared to continue public example notebook validation for 444 / IHES / Megaminx after a publish schema incompatibility was reproduced in the public publish path (`result.model.format` mismatch).


## 2026-07-22
- Accepted the exact SM75 / 2xT4 transformer GEMM bundle after broad and focused Kaggle sweeps plus an integrated Stream1->2->3 gate. The selected T4 policy is QKV m128n128/swizzle8, FF1 m128n128 warp64x32/stages2, and fused attention-output/FF2 m128n128/swizzle2 at `b_micro=384`, concurrency 1. It improved isolated throughput by 84.51-86.44% and integrated throughput by 81.22-84.46%, with 40/40 paired wins in each gate and byte-exact full dumps. A combined attention follow-up was rejected at only +0.53%/+2.21% integrated. Corrected the SM75 QKV contract to its actual CUTLASS default two-stage mainloop and expanded the transformer benchmark sweep so requested small shapes cannot silently emit no row, and allowed the measured FF1 warp64x32 winner in the exact schema-v3 hardware/workload cache. Final local CTest passed 18/18 and retained the canonical SM86 full-score SHA. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_transformer_sm75_2xt4_final_gate_2026-07-22.md`.
- Added fail-closed SM75 CUTLASS GEMM tuning candidates for QKV, FF1, attention-output, and FF2 while preserving existing defaults. Turing FF1 and fused residual epilogues use the supported two-stage pipeline; unsupported SM75 stages/swizzles are rejected. SM75 build passed, Docker CTest passed 18/18, and the idle RTX 3070 regression retained the canonical full-score SHA. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_transformer_sm75_gemm_policy_build_gate_2026-07-22.md`.
- Ran private Kaggle 2xT4 transformer benchmark v20 against tag `stream1-transformer-rtx3070-final-18e5996` / commit `18e5996`. All 80 complete dumps matched SHA-256 `6524c19f...644ef`; both T4s selected `b_micro=384`, concurrency 1. Exact final-CLS-only reached 1,490,199.5 aggregate candidates/s, 1.2251x full-final and 1.2168x the older v19 aggregate. Q=1 q32 added only 0.86% and all other attention/LN candidates regressed or were neutral, so the next boundary is SM75-specific GEMM policy support. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_transformer_kaggle_benchmark_v20_sm75_2026-07-22.md`.
- Closed the profiler-supported RTX 3070 transformer contour after the selected exact bundle's 4.73% isolated and 7.07% integrated gains. Subsequent exact GEMM, FMHA, LayerNorm, layout, cache, and launch-geometry candidates either regressed or stayed below the 3% gate; the last isolated 4.135% launch-overlap win became only 0.720% in Stream1->2->3. Work now moves to an independently profiled SM75 / 2xT4 contour. Evidence: `test_results/local3070_transformer_saturation_2026-07-22.md`.
- Rejected changing the RTX 3070 production transformer launch geometry from `512 x 1` to `256 x 2`. The isolated transformer gate was byte-exact and improved 4.135% with 20/20 paired wins, but the valid ring-2 Stream1->2->3 integration improved only 0.720% with 4/8 wins; `512 x 2` regressed integrated throughput about 6.2%. Production remains `B_MICRO=512`, concurrency 1, ring slot 1. Evidence: `test_results/local3070_transformer_launch_geometry_gate_2026-07-22.md`.
- Rejected a combined RTX 3070 small-tile bundle after replacing a game-contaminated 498-second series with short bookended-idle jobs. FF1 `m64n128/s2/sw4` improved 1.808%, the residual small-tile pair improved 2.258%, and the combined path improved 2.744% with 19/20 wins, but remained below the predeclared 3% gate. All 120 clean complete dumps across the swizzle and combined gates matched the canonical SHA. A vector-4 FF1 epilogue also failed to reduce the resource limit and regressed. Temporary CUTLASS policies were removed; CTest passed 18/18 and production returned to 8.0521 ms exact. Evidence: `test_results/local3070_ff1_small_tile_combined_gate_2026-07-22.md`.
- Rejected CUTLASS stages=4 for RTX 3070 QKV/FF1 after discarding the earlier game-contaminated series and repeating on a bookended-idle GPU. All 48 complete dumps matched the canonical SHA, but QKV4 regressed 5.00%, FF1-4 regressed 19.83%, and both regressed 25.30%, each with 0/12 paired wins. NCU 2025.1.1 showed dynamic shared memory rising 49.15 to 65.54 KiB/CTA and theoretical occupancy halving 16.67% to 8.33%; the temporary policy was removed. SM75/SM86 rebuild passed, CTest passed 18/18, and post-rollback production remained exact at 8.0548 ms. Evidence: `test_results/local3070_qkv_ff1_stage4_gate_2026-07-22.md`.
- Rejected an exact vectorized head-major Q/K/V packing candidate on the idle RTX 3070. All 42 complete dumps matched the canonical SHA, but packed layout regressed the full-path median 10.79% with only 1/20 paired wins. Nsight Systems showed unchanged FMHA median (`224.1455 us` strided vs `224.1910 us` packed) plus `219.5185 us` pack cost per layer, so there is no measured case for folding this layout into the QKV epilogue for the current CUTLASS FMHA. All candidate code was removed; CTest passed 18/18 and production returned to 8.0635 ms exact. Evidence: `test_results/local3070_attention_layout_gate_2026-07-22.md`.
- Rejected byte-exact RTX 3070 GEMM follow-ups after clean Nsight gates: LayerNorm-to-FF1 mainloop fusion regressed 21.6% with 73.7% more instructions, QKV stages2 regressed 1.77% without an occupancy gain, residual `m128n256` regressed 5.88% with 0/20 wins, and the best small residual-tile mix (`AttentionOut m64n128` stages2 plus `FF2 m64n64` stages3) improved 2.278% with 19/20 wins but stayed below the predeclared 3% acceptance gate. Game-contaminated timings were discarded; all accepted measurements began after explicit idle checks. All rejected code was removed, CTest passed 18/18, production measured 8.0702 ms, and the canonical score SHA remained exact. Evidence: `test_results/local3070_gemm_followup_rejections_2026-07-22.md`.
- Rejected the remaining small RTX 3070 memory-policy candidates after byte-exact gates: QKV/FF1 swizzle 4 was neutral, LayerNorm dtype specialization was 0.024% noise, FMHA vector-4 alignment regressed about 1.4%, exact32 produced only +0.289% with 17/30 wins, and an attention-context L2 persistence window regressed about 1.14%. The benchmark-only L2 code and LN specialization were removed, post-rollback exact smoke passed, and the production schema-v3 cache remains unchanged. The next material path is exact LayerNorm-to-GEMM mainloop fusion. Evidence: `test_results/local3070_memory_followup_gates_2026-07-22.md`.
- Accepted an exact-signature RTX 3070 transformer bundle combining QKV/FF1 identity swizzle 8 with exact fused attention-output/FF2 epilogues and identity swizzle 2. The complete bundle preserved the canonical score SHA in 60/60 dumps, improved Stream1 median 4.73% with 30/30 paired wins, and improved Stream1→2→3 median 7.07% with 20/20 paired wins. Nsight confirmed residual GEMM group time -4.38% and lower DRAM pressure at unchanged registers/occupancy. Added atomic fail-closed schema-v3 hardware environment caching. Racecheck was clean, Python contracts passed 15/15, and Docker CTest passed 18/18. Evidence: `test_results/local3070_residual_epilogue_swizzle_bundle_gate_2026-07-22.md`.
- Profiled all selected large GEMMs with NCU 2025.1.1 and rejected two FF1 resource variants plus a QKV `m256n128` reuse tile. FF1 stages2 regressed 0.83%, FF1 warp64x32 regressed 7.13%, and QKV m256n128 regressed 2.72%; all complete dumps remained exact. Current kernels are limited by only about 16% occupancy and 75-91% scheduler cycles with no eligible warp. Evidence: `test_results/local3070_ff1_resource_policy_gate_2026-07-22.md` and `test_results/local3070_gemm_ncu_qkv_reuse_gate_2026-07-22.md`.
- Tested LayerNorm row scheduling while preserving the fused bias/round/LN contract. A faster one-warp-per-row kernel and persistent parameter hoist changed the full score SHA and were rejected; exact two-row and persistent-no-hoist variants were neutral/slower (-0.05% and -0.81%). Production remains one 128-thread CTA per row, while exact alternatives remain opt-in for future hardware-specific autotuning. Evidence: `test_results/local3070_layernorm_row_scheduling_gate_2026-07-22.md`. Final production verification passed Docker CTest 18/18 and retained the canonical full-score SHA.
- Added and exactness-gated a fail-closed CUTLASS FMHA max-K policy for the fixed head-dim-32 backend. `exact32` preserved all 80 complete dumps and improved isolated FMHA 370.34 us to 362.43 us, but its paired full-Stream1 gain was only 0.72%; pairing it with q32k64 regressed 0.12%. Production remains `q64k64+padded64`. Evidence: `test_results/local3070_fmha_maxk_gate_2026-07-22.md`.
- Refreshed the current RTX 3070 Nsight Systems profile with CUDA Graph node tracing. GEMMs now consume 72.5% of GPU kernel time (27.9% residual, 24.0% FF1, 20.6% QKV), versus 11.7% bias-round-LayerNorm and 11.0% FMHA, so the next target is GEMM staging/occupancy rather than further sub-percent attention tweaks. Evidence: `test_results/local3070_transformer_current_profile_2026-07-22.md`.
- Profiled and A/B-tested a LayerNorm split-shared-slot candidate that removes one block barrier while preserving the reduction order. Although the isolated kernel improved from 226.24 us to 224.19 us in NCU 2025.1.1, 20 alternating full-Stream1 pairs were neutral/slower (`8.3263 ms` baseline versus `8.3432 ms` split; 9/20 wins), so the safe barrier implementation remains production default. All 42 complete dumps matched SHA-256 `a949501...a8569d94`, split racecheck was clean, production revalidation retained the same SHA, and Docker CTest passed 18/18. Evidence: `test_results/local3070_layernorm_split_slots_ab_2026-07-22.md`.

## 2026-07-18
- Added the first shape/GPU-aware Stream1 transformer FF1 autotune contour: a versioned full-score dump contract, fail-closed Python selection/cache logic, canonical GPU/model/GEMM-shape signatures, exact full-buffer comparison, repeated determinism checks, and a 3% median speed gate. CPU contract tests cover cache/signature/rejection behavior.
- Local RTX 3070 full-buffer validation found the existing explicit `baseline` path is itself intermittently nondeterministic at both concurrency 1 and 2 and in graph and eager modes. Compute Sanitizer `initcheck` and `memcheck` reported no memory errors, while `racecheck` reported read/write hazards in specialized LayerNorm kernels. Two synchronization experiments were measured and rejected because they did not restore determinism; baseline remains the only default and no tuned candidate is accepted.
- The autotune cache for the current FP16 p900 signature records `baseline_nondeterministic`, so the faster experimental `m64n128` FF1 tile is blocked regardless of throughput until layer-level parity isolates and fixes the pre-existing race.

The new transformer-specific view/scratch API runs p900 parent rows through fast input token construction, input/output LayerNorm, four pre-norm attention/FFN blocks, CUTLASS linear projections, a fused row-wise seq_len=51 attention kernel, SiLU FFN activation, residual adds, and score-key quantization. Existing MLP/dispatcher tests were updated for the expanded MLP view initializer shape. Verification recorded in `test_results/stream1_transformer_forward_2026-06-29.md`.
- Hardened the Stream1 piece-transformer loader quality gates. Manifest fallback u32 parsing now only falls back when the primary key is absent and rejects conflicting primary/fallback values, runner and benchmark entry points fail closed before transformer runtime sizing/allocation because forward dispatch is not wired, and Stream1 weight/scratch allocation paths free partial CUDA allocations on exceptions. Verification recorded in `test_results/stream1_transformer_loader_2026-06-29.md`.
- Added the Stream1 piece-transformer host loader path and scratch arena scaffolding without wiring transformer inference. The weight loader now parses and validates the exact p900 piece-transformer manifest contract, loads exported transformer tensors and piece layout tables with exact byte sizes, exposes host/device/scratch byte helpers, and keeps MLP loading on an explicit separate branch. Verification recorded in `test_results/stream1_transformer_loader_2026-06-29.md`.
- Added Stream1 piece-transformer export tooling: an auto-detecting tools/export_stream1.py wrapper and tools/export_stream1_transformer.py for Kaggle p900 PilgrimPieceTransformer checkpoints. The exporter writes fast-slot input tables, piece layout tensors, Transformer block tensors, manifests with backend=piece_transformer, rejects non-24-output, non-4-layer, and non-1024-FF variants, and supports optional CPU fp32 reference JSON generation. Verification recorded in test_results/stream1_transformer_exporter_2026-06-29.md.
- Added tagged Stream1 model backend config constants and transformer metadata fields, preserving existing MLP defaults. Stream1 row-mode helpers now force the piece-transformer backend to parent rows while keeping MLP one-output child-row behavior, with contract coverage recorded in `test_results/stream1_piece_transformer_task1_2026-06-29.md`. Unknown Stream1 backend values now fail closed in row-mode helpers, MLP weight validation, and manifest backend parsing; legacy manifests without `backend` still default to MLP.

## 2026-06-20
- Added `BEAM_SOLVE_BUCKET_MODE` for ordinary solve-to-center runs that keep
  searching after the first hit. Stream2 can now record solved candidates
  without setting `stop_flag`; the runner completes the hit depth, completes
  `BEAM_SOLVE_BUCKET_EXTRA_DEPTHS` more depths, gathers solved buckets from all
  ranks, reconstructs every stored solution, and writes per-length summaries.
- Added `hpc/ihes_cube_model/ihes_solve_bucket.sh`, a 8-GPU IHES launcher that
  selects known length-23 puzzles from `solv_uniq.csv`, runs original and
  reflected solve-bucket passes, and writes a compact TSV with the best found
  original-facing solution for each variant.
- Changed resident solution-repair task ordering to process higher puzzle ids
  first. Parsed solution rows are now stably sorted by descending `puzzle_id`,
  so `1000, 999, ...` are prioritized while preserving per-puzzle solution
  order.
- Added an explicit `BEAM_STREAM1_MODE=uniform` runtime mode for repair/debug
  runs. In this mode Stream1 model inference is not launched; the captured
  ring-slot graph fills the score ring with zero scores so selection behaves as
  an unscored BFS frontier subject only to the configured beam width and
  dedup/final materialization.
- Fixed distributed repair result reconstruction so rank 0 is always the
  reconstruction controller after a global solved stop. Previously rank 0 could
  write `segment_solved=1` with `segment_len=-1` when another rank owned the
  solved candidate, which made resident repair TSV aggregation report false
  misses or false deltas.

## 2026-06-13
- Made the path-trace debugger rank-aware for distributed runs. After each
  local prefix scan, ranks exchange tiny debug packets with NCCL so only the
  rank that owns the tracked prefix continues issuing the next tracked move.
- Fixed the path-trace prefinal hash scanner to store its temporary block
  indices in the survivor-sized final select scratch instead of the final
  request buffer, which is not a valid scratch target on the multi-rank final
  materialization path.
- Made MEPhI CMake debug flags in `mephi_8xa100_common.sh` overridable from
  the SLURM environment, so path tracing can be compiled for targeted
  diagnostics without changing the default fast launcher behavior.
- Added the `+3.0` score offset for Stream1 single-output model quantization so
  models trained with outputs starting at `-3` are shifted into the nonnegative
  runtime score-key range before clamping.
- Enabled the non-LayerNorm Stream1 CUTLASS runtime path to consume bf16
  exported weights. The post-GEMM bias/ReLU and residual add kernels now use
  dtype-aware scalar loads/stores, while the existing fp16 half2 fast path is
  unchanged.
- Added an early SM80+ guard for bf16 Stream1 inference so bf16 runs fail
  before kernel launch on GPUs that cannot execute the CUTLASS bf16 path.

## 2026-06-12
- Started compile-time puzzle state specialization. CMake now infers
  `BEAM_STATE_LOGICAL_BYTES` from `data/puzzle_info.json` by default, computes
  aligned `BEAM_STATE_PHYSICAL_BYTES`, and passes state size/alignment into all
  targets as compile definitions.
- Replaced the fixed `State128` storage definition with a configurable
  `StatePacked` alias, while preserving the existing `State128` type name for
  incremental compatibility. Final-response temporary metadata now uses the
  first padding bytes after `STATE_LEN` instead of hard-coded `v[120]`.
- Made final materialization state buffers align to `alignof(State128)` /
  `alignof(FinalResponse)`, and made the old batchnorm-folded Stream1 exporter
  accept configurable `--num-classes`.
- Added a CMake-script state-sizing smoke check that verifies the current
  megaminx shape remains `120` logical bytes / `128` physical bytes and that
  exact-alignment state lengths still reserve padding for finalization metadata.
- Extended compile-time puzzle specialization to infer `BEAM_MOVE_COUNT` from
  `puzzle_info.json`, keeping megaminx at 24 moves and allowing IHES cube to
  compile with 18 moves.
- Made `production_runner` read generators/move names from either the old
  `actions`/`names` JSON or top-level `generators` JSON, and added
  `BEAM_GENERATOR_PATH` so non-megaminx runs can use `data/puzzle_info.json`
  directly.
- Added `hpc/ihes_cube_smoke/`, a GitHub-tracked MEPhI SLURM smoke package for
  `cayleypy-ihes-cube`. It downloads Kaggle data, creates dummy 1-output fp16
  weights, compiles with `STATE_LEN=72` / `MOVE_COUNT=18`, and runs a tiny
  single-GPU pipeline check.
- Standardized tracked HPC SLURM scripts to a `24:00:00` walltime and documented
  dependency-based IHES smoke submission so new checks can wait for existing
  jobs instead of cancelling them.

## 2026-06-11
- Added `hpc/portable_8xa100_80gb/`, a portable 8xA100 80GB SLURM launch
  package for `BEAM_WIDTH=1400000000` with `SHARD_COUNT=64`. The launcher runs
  the full original-plus-reflected solve workflow and uses per-job build/history
  directories to avoid cross-job collisions.
- Made `hpc/mephi_8xa100_common.sh` support both the MEPhI `workdir/repo`
  layout and a normal cloned repository root, while keeping cleanup constrained
  to known build/history child directories.
- Made NCCL include/library paths auto-detect from the active venv's
  `nvidia.nccl` package unless explicitly provided.
- Changed `hpc/solve_then_reflect.sh` to source its common script from explicit
  `BEAM_COMMON_SH` when provided; no fallback path is used.
- Ignored portable run artifacts (`build-a100*`, `history*`) and `core.*`
  dumps in `.gitignore`.

## 2026-06-05
- Prepared Kaggle and Molab experiment artifacts for GitHub publication:
  updated the main Kaggle notebooks for multi-model Stream1 test selection,
  added torchrun Kaggle package variants, added Molab probe scripts, and
  preserved local push snapshot folders for reproducibility.
- Updated MEPhI 8xA100 launch/tuning defaults to use the cluster-measured
  Stream1 winner: `BEAM_B_MICRO=8192`, `BEAM_STREAM1_CONCURRENCY=8`, and
  `BEAM_STREAM3_RING_SLOTS=8`. The staged and Cartesian tuners now default the
  Stream3 ring-slot sweep to `8`, and tuner launchers clamp ring slots upward
  when a sourced `best_stream1.env` requests higher Stream1 concurrency.
- Reduced MEPhI tuning SLURM time limits in the repository to `02:00:00` for

## 2026-06-04
- Added `hpc/tune_8xa100_staged.sh`, a staged MEPhI tuner that optimizes one
  parameter family at a time instead of running the full Cartesian pipeline
  sweep. The stages are shard count, Stream3 ring slots, Stream4 batch,
  Stream4 trigger multiplier, final materialization chunk, and final exchange
  scale; each stage writes the current best config to `logs/best_pipeline.env`.
- Added `BEAM_FINAL_MATERIALIZE_EXCHANGE_SCALE_PPM` and changed final
  materialization exchange slot sizing from `final_chunk * WORLD_SIZE` to
  `max(final_chunk, final_chunk * scale_ppm / 1e6)`. The final exchange path
  still exchanges counts first and fails before payload copy if actual receive
  totals exceed the scaled slot capacity. MEPhI defaults use `1200000`.
- Changed the MEPhI pipeline tuning default final materialization sweep to
  `65536 131072 262144 524288` so `best_pipeline.env` selects a measured
  memory/speed tradeoff instead of preserving the old Stream3-sized final
  chunk by default. Verified shell syntax with Docker `bash:5.2`.
- Added `BEAM_FINAL_MATERIALIZE_CHUNK_CANDIDATES` to decouple final materialization chunk capacity from `STREAM3_BATCH_CANDIDATES`. The default `0` preserves the old behavior; MEPhI pipeline tuning can now sweep `FINAL_MATERIALIZE_CHUNK_SWEEP`, records `final_chunk` in the summary, and writes the winning value into `best_pipeline.env`.
- Prepared MEPhI HPC 8xA100 SLURM launch artifacts for the multi-GPU
  `production_runner` beam search: `hpc/start_8xa100_beamsearch.sh`,
  `hpc/README_8xa100.md`, and verification note
  `test_results/mephi_8xa100_launch_prep_2026-06-04.md`. The script targets
  `puzzle=992`, `depth=80`, global `beam=260000000`, `WORLD_SIZE=8`,
  `sm_80`, K1 radius `5`, static-hybrid history, and torchrun `--no-python`.
  Per MEPhI rules, no hidden cluster submission was performed; submission must
  go through the visible `ssh mephi` -> `ssh basis` gateway with
  `sbatch -p kaf12 start.sh`.
- Updated the MEPhI 8xA100 script per user follow-up: initial run depth is now
  `12`, and the script clones/updates GitHub
  `https://github.com/TryDotAtwo/MultiGPUBeamSearch.git` branch `main` into
  the job directory before building.
- Added guarded cleanup to the MEPhI script: it removes only the resolved
  per-job build directory, removes the resolved per-job history directory only
  on successful completion, keeps history after failures, and never touches the
  repository, logs, SLURM output, prediction stats, or shared CUTLASS checkout.
  After cluster validation, the script now uses `#SBATCH --ntasks=1`,
  `#SBATCH --gres=gpu:8`, and `#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=8`, and avoids all
  network operations inside the compute job because `t2n1` cannot resolve
  `github.com`; repo/CUTLASS preparation is done from the visible `basis`
  shell before `sbatch`.

- Added `global_spill_count` to fixed `layout_streams` memory and wired the Stream 3 graph template as threshold/dedup -> restore/owner/split -> local-pending collector.

Guard scans still show no active `smoke/fallback/torch/PyTorch`, no dispatcher `cudaMalloc/cudaFree`, and atomics only in Stream 2 solved path.
- Added Stream 3 global spill drain CUDA kernel. The collector now drains `global_spill_buffer` into free shard dirty regions before collecting new local pending candidates, compacting still-blocked spill entries in-place.
- Updated Stream 3 graph template order to threshold/dedup -> restore/owner/split -> global spill drain -> local pending collector.
- Extended Stream 3 CUDA tests to verify global spill drain count, dirty count, and survivor write. Docker CUDA/CUTLASS tests after global spill drain: 10/10 passed. Guard scans still show no active `smoke/fallback/torch/PyTorch`, no dispatcher `cudaMalloc/cudaFree`, and atomics only in Stream 2 solved path.
- Linked NCCL as a required build dependency and replaced Stream 5 test path with `stream5_exchange_nccl_cuda`. The current validated NCCL contract covers single-rank communicator behavior; multi-rank launcher remains pending.
- Added Stream 3 remote receive collector. Remote receive ranges from `remote_recv_buffer` are routed into shard dirty regions or `global_spill_buffer` without atomics.
- Added CUDA threshold/final primitives: local score histogram, threshold select, final threshold filter, load-balance target assignment, final candidate buffer, and final request build.
- Added CPU history reconstruction module and tests. CPU history now stores per-depth `CandidateMeta` vectors and reconstructs root-to-goal move sequences from solved metadata.
- Added CUDA-only `production_runner` target with arguments `puzzle_id depth beam`. The runner prints key config values, GPU total/free memory before start, static allocation size, layout sizes, GPU free memory after static allocation, elapsed time, and average depth time.
- Optimized Stream 4 shard job table clear: empty shards now skip table-state clearing inside the GPU path, and non-empty shards use a parallel clear kernel before fixed-capacity dedup.
- Verification after these changes: Docker CUDA/CUTLASS/NCCL tests passed 12/12. Guard scans show no active `smoke/fallback/torch/PyTorch`, no dispatcher `cudaMalloc/cudaFree`, and atomics only in Stream 2 solved path.
- Short production runner check: `production_runner 0 1 4096` passed. Before empty-shard skip, average depth time was 12.2484 sec; after unconditional parallel clear it was 14.5056 sec; after empty-shard skip it was 2.12884 sec.

- Replaced Stream 3 single-thread insertion sort with no-atomic parallel odd-even compare/swap passes over arbitrary `STREAM3_BATCH_CANDIDATES`.
- Replaced Stream 3 single-thread dedup with no-atomic parallel unique-head marking, block scan, and head compaction. Payload contract remains original candidate index, not compact index.
- Replaced Stream 4 shard-job serial fixed hash-table insert/dump path with no-atomic threshold marking, parallel compact, bitonic sort over power-of-two capacity, parallel unique-head marking, block scan, and unique compaction.
- Added fixed Stream 4 per-shard block-count/block-offset scratch into `layout_streams`.
- Added NCCL histogram AllReduce wrapper and final keep-count AllGather wrapper. Single-rank NCCL collective contracts pass in Docker.

- Production runner short check after parallel hot-path work: `production_runner 0 1 4096` passed with `avg_depth_sec=0.0941149`.
- Nsight Systems profile after parallel hot-path work saved to `test_results/nsys_production_runner_after_parallel_2026-05-22.nsys-rep` and `.sqlite`. Profiled run reported `avg_depth_sec=0.0950039`; NVTX summary showed dispatcher depth range about 94.976 ms, Stream 3 threshold/sort/dedup about 18.506 ms total, and Stream 4 shard jobs about 16.349 ms total.
- Split Stream 5 NCCL exchange into explicit architecture phases: `stream5_exchange_counts_nccl_cuda`, host recv-offset preparation, and `stream5_exchange_payload_nccl_cuda`. The combined wrapper remains a single-rank convenience test path; multi-rank code must use the two-phase path so payload receive sizes are known after count exchange.
- Extended `production_runner` arguments to `production_runner <puzzle_id> <depth> <beam> [world_size] [local_rank]`; validation requires `world_size in [1,255]` and `local_rank < world_size`.

- Ran production runner static/runtime validation for `WORLD_SIZE=2,LOCAL_RANK=1` and `WORLD_SIZE=100,LOCAL_RANK=99` with depth 0. Both paths initialized, instantiated graph templates, printed memory layout, and exited successfully.

- Removed the explicit `WORLD_SIZE=1/2/100` config/static-memory test loops and report markers. `WORLD_SIZE` remains a regular config parameter used by alignment, send/recv arrays, final send/recv arrays, Stream 3 owner routing, Stream 5 exchange, and final load-balancing paths.
- Replaced the Stream 3 odd-even sort path with architecture-preferred CUB/fixed-temp mechanics: threshold/scan/compact, fixed-count `cub::DeviceMergeSort::SortPairs` over `Hash128`, `cub::DeviceReduce::ReduceByKey` with min packed `(score_key << 32) | payload_id`, and sentinel-tail filtering back into `unique_key/unique_val/unique_count`.
- Added fixed pre-start Stream 3 CUB temp storage into `layout_streams` and `StaticMemoryPlan`; no Stream 3 hot-path allocation was added.
- Removed the Stream 4 bitonic-sort shard-job path and removed Stream 4 per-shard block-count/block-offset scratch. Stream 4 shard job now uses the approved fixed-capacity hash-table dedup semantics again: threshold plus Hash128 dedup plus best `CandidateMeta` by `score_key`, `parent_idx`, and `route_packed`; table state is cleared inside the fixed-capacity job.
- Verification after architecture cleanup: Docker CUDA/CUTLASS/NCCL tests passed 12/12. Guard scans show no active `smoke/fallback/torch/PyTorch/odd_even/bitonic/world_size_1_2_100`, no dispatcher `cudaMalloc/cudaFree`, and atomics only in Stream 2 solved path.

- Updated project rule in `AGENTS.md`: Stream 4 production path is now threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; Stream 4 hash-table dedup is removed.
- Added `stream4_active_sort_slots` config with default `1`. Static memory now allocates Stream 4 CUB scratch per active sort slot, not per shard: sort keys, reduce keys, sort values, reduce values, keep flags, block counts, block offsets, scratch count, and CUB temp.
- Removed active Stream 4 hash-table CUDA code and scratch fields. `stream4_shard_job_cuda` now performs threshold marking, compact-before-sort, CUB `DeviceMergeSort::SortPairs`, CUB `DeviceReduce::ReduceByKey`, valid-unique compaction, clean count writeback, dirty count reset, and processing flag reset.
- Dispatcher now instantiates Stream 4 graph templates for `SHARD_COUNT * stream4_active_sort_slots`; the current sequential dispatcher launches slot 0 while future production scheduling can choose a free slot.

- Added real Stream 4 sort-slot resource management. Dispatcher creates one CUDA stream and one completion event per `stream4_active_sort_slots`; runtime scheduler now acquires an idle slot, queries completion events, waits only when all sort slots are busy, launches the shard-slot graph template, records the slot completion event, and drains only busy slots.
- Added fixed pre-start Stream 3 scratch arrays for parallel owner routing and collector scans: `stream3_owner`, `stream3_shard_counts`, `stream3_shard_offsets`, `stream3_spill_counts`, and `stream3_spill_offsets`.
- Parallelized Stream 3 restore/owner/split: restored `CandidateMeta` entries in parallel, counted candidates per owner with one block per peer, scanned `send_count` into `send_offset`, and scattered local/remote candidates into owned regions without atomics.
- Parallelized Stream 3 local-pending and remote-recv collectors: counted candidates per shard with one block per shard, scanned shard/spill counts, scattered into shard dirty regions or `global_spill_buffer`, and preserved the single Stream 3 writer contract for shard buffers.

- Replaced single `global_spill_buffer` with ping-pong spill buffers: `global_spill_buffer_a`, `global_spill_buffer_b`, `global_spill_count[2]`, and `global_spill_active_index`, all inside fixed pre-start `layout_streams` memory.
- Reworked Stream 3 global spill drain from in-place serial compaction to no-atomic parallel ping-pong drain: active spill buffer is counted by shard, accepted candidates scatter into shard dirty regions, still-blocked candidates compact into inactive spill buffer, and active index flips after scatter.
- Updated Stream 3 local-pending and remote-recv collectors to append spills to the active ping-pong spill buffer selected by `global_spill_active_index`.

- Added device-resident `threshold_initialized` flag and periodic threshold update semantics. Periodic update builds the clean-survivor histogram, copies the single-GPU local histogram to the global histogram, and applies monotonic threshold initialization/update: keep `UINT32_MAX` while total survivors are below beam, then only tighten threshold.
- Changed Stream 3 and Stream 4 CUDA Graph templates to read `current_threshold` from device memory at execution time instead of capturing `UINT32_MAX` at graph instantiation.
- Added single-GPU final depth wiring: final histogram, final global threshold selection, final filter/load-balance, final request count readback, final materialize, `next_frontier_states_tmp -> current_frontier_states`, and threshold reset for the next depth.
- Adjusted final scratch layout so final candidate/request/response buffers are placed after the active `layout_streams` byte range. This prevents final filter/load-balance writes from corrupting clean `survivor_shard` data before materialization starts.

Stream4 job count `1088` comes from `64 shards * ~17 waves` at full beam.
- Added production-runner tuning env knobs and memory overlay logs. `BEAM_B_MICRO`, `BEAM_STREAM3_RING_SLOTS`, `BEAM_RING_COUNT`, `BEAM_SHARD_COUNT`, `BEAM_STREAM4_ACTIVE_SORT_SLOTS`, `BEAM_STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES`, `BEAM_GLOBAL_THRESHOLD_UPDATE_PERIOD_SHARDS`, `BEAM_GLOBAL_SPILL_CAPACITY`, and `BEAM_SOLVED_RESULT_CAPACITY` can override defaults without source edits.
