- Added opt-in `BEAM_STREAM3_BATCH_CANDIDATES` for manual 8xA100 runs and Stream1+2+3 pipeline smoke. This decouples transformer Stream1 microbatch sizing from Stream3 aggregation: `B_MICRO=512` can now use a large Stream3 batch such as `1572864` candidates (`512 * 24 * 128`) instead of the default `98304`, reducing excessive Stream3 job counts without changing default behavior. Generated best-env files preserve the explicit Stream3 batch override. Manual runtime autodetection now derives `ring_count` from the requested staging slots and explicit Stream3 batch, so `B_MICRO=512`, requested slots `8`, and `STREAM3_BATCH_CANDIDATES=1572864` derive `ring_count=14` instead of inflating physical ring staging to `223 * 128` slots. Shell preflight and C++ runtime both require divisibility by `effective_parent_batch * MOVE_COUNT`; Docker verification built `stream_pipeline_benchmark` and `production_runner`, passed `contract_tests`, and validated the good/bad formula cases. Evidence: `test_results/stream3_batch_override_2026-07-06.md`.
- Restored the Stream1 piece-transformer production granularity to the MLP-style external ring-slot contract. `BEAM_B_MICRO` now remains the full parent rows per dispatcher ring-slot for Stream2/3, while new `BEAM_STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_MICRO` controls only internal native PieceTransformer Stream1 slicing and scratch size. The transformer fills the existing score ring in offset chunks; Stream2 still runs once over the full external slot. Pipeline smoke defaults now use target `B_MICRO`/concurrency rather than isolated Stream1 microbatch size. Verification built `production_runner`, `stream_pipeline_benchmark`, and tests in Docker, passed full `ctest` 13/13, and ran real-weight macro/micro pipeline smokes for `B_MICRO=1024,micro=512` and `B_MICRO=8192,micro=512`. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_transformer_macro_micro_ring_slot_fix_2026-07-06.md`.
- Fixed the MEPhI Megaminx transformer pipeline smoke launcher so stale inherited `BEAM_STREAM3_BATCH_CANDIDATES` cannot shrink the effective ring-slot count below `BEAM_STREAM1_CONCURRENCY`. `run_pipeline_smoke_config` now computes and passes a smoke-local Stream3 batch as `b_micro * 24 * BEAM_STREAM3_RING_SLOTS` unless `PIPELINE_STREAM3_BATCH_CANDIDATES` is explicitly set. Local Docker smoke passed for `stream123`, `B_MICRO=8192`, `BEAM_STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_MICRO=512`, `concurrency=8`, `stream3_batch=1572864`. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_transformer_macro_micro_ring_slot_fix_2026-07-06.md`.
- Fixed the MEPhI Megaminx transformer full target launcher so stale inherited `BEAM_STREAM3_BATCH_CANDIDATES` cannot reduce effective Stream3 ring slots below Stream1 concurrency. `run_full_config` now sets a run-local Stream3 batch as `b_micro * 24 * ring_slots` before manual config derivation/validation, while preserving an explicit `FULL_STREAM3_BATCH_CANDIDATES` override for intentional custom runs. This addresses cluster job 31758 (`invalid_stream1_concurrency=8 stream3_effective_ring_slots=4`). Verification: Docker `bash -n` passed. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_transformer_macro_micro_ring_slot_fix_2026-07-06.md`.
- Fixed a false GPU-budget rejection for the Megaminx PieceTransformer full run. `estimate_non_static_device_bytes` now estimates PieceTransformer Stream1 scratch with `BEAM_STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_MICRO` instead of external `B_MICRO`, matching the actual production allocation path. This addresses cluster job 31759, where the manual config guard estimated `required=58.5GB` by pretending transformer scratch was allocated for `B_MICRO=8192` instead of `micro=512`. MLP estimates remain unchanged. Verification: Docker full `ctest` passed 13/13, and real-weight `stream123` pipeline smoke passed for `B_MICRO=8192`, `BEAM_STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_MICRO=512`, `concurrency=8`, `stream3_batch=1572864`. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_transformer_macro_micro_ring_slot_fix_2026-07-06.md`.
- Added an exact final-CLS-only optimization for the native Stream1 `piece_transformer` p900 block51 path. The last transformer layer still runs full 51-token QKV and full FMHA attention, then gathers the CLS row and runs attention-output projection, LN2, FFN, output LN, and logits only for CLS while preserving the old block51 half-rounding order (`linear_residual -> bias_add -> layernorm_copy`). Added `BEAM_STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_FINAL_CLS_ONLY` and logs/MEPhI script propagation. MLP and generic transformer paths remain untouched; no fallback/distillation behavior was added. Verification: Docker full `ctest` passed 13/13, real-weight checksums/digests match full final-layer mode, and local graph smoke improved `512x1` from `286690.4` to `358039.7` candidates/s. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_transformer_final_cls_only_2026-07-07.md`.

- 2026-07-08: Added opt-in `BEAM_DEBUG_RING_GRAPH_SYNC=1` diagnostics for windowed ring-slot CUDA Graph launches. The dispatcher now synchronizes the lane stream after each windowed graph launch only when the env var is enabled and prints rank/ring/slot/job/template_job/parent_base/count before throwing the CUDA error. Default production behavior is unchanged. Verified Docker build of `production_runner` and `stream_pipeline_benchmark` in `gpu-dev-cutlass-nsight:cuda128-sm120`; evidence in `test_results/ring_graph_debug_sync_2026-07-08.md`.

- 2026-07-08: Raised the default final materialize exchange capacity policy to `WORLD_SIZE_EFFECTIVE * 1000000` ppm for MEPhI launchers and native runtime config. On 8 ranks this defaults to `BEAM_FINAL_MATERIALIZE_EXCHANGE_SCALE_PPM=8000000`, matching the observed requirement after rank 7 failed with `exchange recv total exceeds device capacity` at 2x. Explicit env overrides are still honored.
- Optimized the native CUDA/CUTLASS Stream1 `piece_transformer` p900 block51 path for SM80+ tensor-core use. The fp16 transformer GEMM wrappers now dispatch to CUTLASS `arch::Sm80` / `GemmShape<16,8,16>` on SM80+ GPUs while preserving SM75 / `16,8,8` on T4. Added a rounded fused `bias + LayerNorm` kernel for block51 and marked hot scalar helpers `__forceinline__`; this fixed a measured intermediate regression from `52k` candidates/s back to `892k` candidates/s locally, then SM80 dispatch raised local graph replay to `955065.5` candidates/s at `b_micro=512,concurrency=2` with the same score digest as the old binary. Full Docker `ctest` passed 13/13, and local Stream1+2+3 pipeline smoke returned `status=OK`. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_transformer_sm80_tensorcore_dispatch_2026-07-08.md`.
- Added Stream1 piece-transformer final-layer CLS-attention as an explicit CUTLASS FMHA path (`BEAM_STREAM1_TRANSFORMER_FINAL_CLS_ATTENTION=1`) using `num_queries=1` for the final block. It is exact on the local fp16 smoke checksum but slower on RTX 3070, so the HPC benchmark now sweeps it instead of forcing it. Added `BEAM_STREAM1_EXPORT_DTYPE=fp16|bf16` to `hpc/bench_8xa100_megaminx_transformer.sh`, BF16 support to the Python Torch benchmark, and isolated summary/best-env propagation for the final CLS attention flag. Verification: Docker build, `ctest` 13/13, local 3070 fp16 A/B, native BF16 smoke, Torch BF16 smoke, LibTorch BF16 CPU loader smoke. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_transformer_bf16_cls_attention_2026-07-08.md`.
- 2026-07-19: Added full score dumps, checksum, and FNV-1a digest diagnostics to the eager Stream1 MLP benchmark. Fixed an eager-only host crash by constructing `Stream1NetworkView` from stable host-side residual pointer vectors instead of repeatedly indexing CUDA managed pointer tables while kernels are in flight. Production MLP/CUDA Graph code is unchanged. Normal asynchronous RTX 3070 verification passed 20/20 at `B_MICRO=2048,concurrency=1` with one full-buffer SHA-256 and passed concurrency 1/2/4/8/16/32 without `CUDA_LAUNCH_BLOCKING` or fallback behavior. Evidence: `test_results/stream1_backend_determinism_matrix_2026-07-19.md`.

- 2026-07-19: Fixed native CUTLASS transformer nondeterminism caused by a shared-memory WAR race between LayerNorm mean consumption and variance-reduction scratch reuse. Added the required post-mean block barrier to all eight native LayerNorm variants. RTX 3070 verification produced one full-buffer SHA across 100/100 eager and 100/100 CUDA Graph processes, one SHA across both 20-repeat autotuner gates, Compute Sanitizer racecheck reported 0 hazards, Docker CTest passed 15/15, and Python autotuner tests passed 7/7. No fallback, launch blocking, or production MLP change was added. Evidence: `test_results/native_cutlass_layernorm_race_fix_2026-07-19.md`.

- 2026-07-19: Began the post-race-fix native transformer memory optimization plan. Added a tested Nsight SQLite kernel-family summarizer; fresh CUDA Graph node profiling at `b_micro=512,concurrency=1` attributes 46.4% to fused GEMM, 27.6% to plain GEMM, 15.4% to LayerNorm, and 10.4% to FMHA. Expanded the compiled GEMM policy contract by family and added FF1 `m128n128`. Exact 20-run-per-policy full-dump gating selected `m128n128` at median `9.87485 ms` versus baseline `10.41035 ms` (5.14% faster), with one SHA across all 60 runs. Full CTest passed 15/15 and Python tests passed 14/14. Evidence: `test_results/native_cutlass_ff1_3policy_graph_c1_20_2026-07-19.json` and `test_results/local3070_transformer_nsys_postfix_nodes_2026-07-19.nsys-rep`.

- 2026-07-19: Expanded native transformer CUTLASS policies independently for FF1, QKV, attention-out, and FF2. RTX 3070 coordinate sweeps selected `m128n128 / warp 64x64` for all four families; every one of 240 measured full score dumps had the same SHA-256. Selected combined median reached `8.3351 ms` versus the earlier fixed graph baseline `11.1791 ms` (approximately 25.4% faster, with final fresh paired validation pending). Evidence: `test_results/local3070_transformer_multifamily_gemm_sweep_2026-07-19.md`.

- 2026-07-19: Completed the first multi-family GEMM gate: combined FF1/QKV/attention-out/FF2 `m128n128` builds in `production_runner`, full Docker CTest passes 15/15, Python tests pass 15/15, and bounded Compute Sanitizer racecheck reports 0 hazards. The full `b_micro=512` sanitizer benchmark was stopped after six minutes as impractical; normal `b512` exactness is covered by 240 identical full score dumps. Evidence: `test_results/local3070_transformer_multifamily_gemm_sweep_2026-07-19.md`.

## 2026-07-19 — RTX 3070 multi-family GEMM cache v2 and pipeline acceptance

- Added strict schema-v2 multi-family policy resolution and cache-to-environment tooling; any signature/family/policy mismatch atomically returns all-baseline assignments.
- Selected `m128n128` for QKV, attention-output, FF1, and FF2 only for the exact RTX 3070 Laptop GPU / FP16 / b512 / concurrency-1 model signature.
- Preserved the baseline full-score dump byte-for-byte across 240 measured dumps (`a949501...a8569d94`) and retained the selected b8 racecheck result with zero hazards.
- In six alternating Stream1-to-2-to-3 pairs, median latency improved from 103.2725 ms to 83.2296 ms (19.41%); selected won every pair.
- Kept final-CLS outside the independent cache contract until a dedicated runtime dispatch exists; no production MLP behavior was changed.
## 2026-07-19 — full-attention q64k64 versus q32k64 hardware sweep

- Added strict compiled full-attention tile policies `q64k64` and `q32k64`; unset remains the measured q64k64 reference and unknown values throw without fallback.
- Confirmed all 40 complete score dumps were byte-identical to `a949501...a8569d94`.
- Rejected q32k64 on RTX 3070: median Stream1 latency 8.4259 ms versus 8.2158 ms for q64k64 (2.56% slower), consistent with reloading K/V across two query blocks.
- Recorded the Nsight Compute 2025.1.1 / driver 572.70 incompatibility instead of substituting counters, and moved the next implementation target to a dedicated Q=1 CLS kernel.
## 2026-07-22 — NCU recovery and final-CLS attention decision

- Recovered Nsight Compute by explicitly using installed 2025.1.1 instead of incompatible PATH-default 2026.1.1 with driver 572.70.
- Full FMHA is register-limited (125 registers/thread, 33.33% theoretical occupancy) and has 188,347 shared-store bank conflicts; NCU estimates 5.249% shared-store opportunity.
- Added exact tensor-core CLS q32k64 policy. All c1/c2 outputs matched complete baseline dumps.
- Q=1 improved isolated c1 Stream1 by 5.05% but regressed c2 and c1 Stream1-to-2-to-3, so it was not selected. Removed a scalar-warp experiment after its complete dump differed.
- Added and publicly validated a ready-to-run 2xT4 Megaminx puzzle-10 example with attached cayley-py-megaminx inputs and output-1 checkpoint. Requested beam 1024 aligns safely to effective 8192; puzzle 10 solved at depth 10 with a valid length-10 path. Fixed the public ingest endpoint to POST /v1/results, added an explicit safe Kaggle publisher User-Agent, and moved checkout/build scratch under /tmp so Kaggle outputs contain only useful artifacts. Example v3 completed and its result was staged in GitHub; the universal public template was updated to v3. No beam/CUDA architecture changed.

- Added a public Cube4 Transformer 2xT4 notebook package with the exact p002/output-24/ReLU checkpoint contract, arbitrary requested beam selection against nearest 2^10..2^25 profiles, 28 GiB RAM plus 50 GiB disk history budgets, PyTorch-compatible NCCL selection, and the verified generator schema. Private Kaggle evidence includes a 4-move beam-1024 solve in 1.27 s and a 12-move beam-2^16 solve in 8.74 s; the 2^25 depth-8 fit run remains under terminal monitoring. No beam/CUDA algorithm changed.


- 2026-07-30: Extended the public Cube4 2xT4 notebook with explicit first/collect modes. Collect mode finishes the hit depth plus a configurable number of extra depths, allocates a fail-closed per-rank solution bucket (default 2000), gathers and CPU-validates solutions from both ranks, and writes full TSV/CSV plus a separate best-solution CSV. Static-hybrid history now correctly preflights an automatic practical maximum: up to 29.0 GB host RAM while reserving 1.5 GB for Python/LibTorch, plus 50 GiB disk.


- 2026-07-30: Kaggle acceptance v3 completed on real 2xT4. At beam 2^16 and depth 100, collect mode found and CPU-validated 8 distinct length-10 solutions across both ranks and wrote full TSV/CSV plus best CSV. The auto history budget selected 27,497,660,672 bytes from 28,997,660,672 available, reserving 1.5 GB. Separate v13 proved the 2^25 profile fits 2xT4 through depth 8 in 1697.60 seconds, peaking at 7107 MiB per GPU with mean utilization 98.43%/99.16% and no OOM/overflow.

- 2026-07-30: Published Kaggle notebook `trydotatwo/cube4-transformer-2xt4-beam-search`. Public v1 completed on 2xT4: solve time 18.376 seconds at beam 2^16/depth 100, collect mode preserved 8 CPU-validated length-10 solutions (6 from rank 0, 2 from rank 1), and exported full TSV/CSV plus best CSV. Effective history RAM was auto-sized to 27,469,635,840 bytes with 1.5 GB reserved; disk budget was 50 GiB.

- Split public 2xT4 runtime tuning into fail-closed backend registries: MLP uses configs/kaggle_t4_mlp_profiles.json, piece Transformer uses configs/kaggle_t4_transformer_profiles.json, and a registry/backend mismatch aborts before build. The notebook now auto-discovers standard Transformer bundle metadata/generators, forwards optional explicit bundle paths, isolates MLP/Transformer CMake caches, and applies the same user debug controls to both backends.

- 2026-07-31: Accepted the Cube4 Transformer Kaggle 2xT4 `2**26` depth-8 profile: `B_MICRO=384`, 192 logical Stream3 accumulation slots (`1,769,472` candidates), two physical rings, 8 shards, capacity scale 1.0, Stream4 batch/trigger `196,608/393,216`, one active sort slot, and independent final-materialization chunk `88,064`. It completed in `2765.04s`, averaged `99.60%` GPU utilization, peaked at `13,003 MiB/GPU`, and had no OOM/overflow/fatal errors. The `98,304` final-chunk A/B was stable but slower at `3302.62s`. Added profile anchor 26 to the universal backend-specific registry, forwarded bounded-ring/final-chunk controls, fixed history-budget derivation order (29.0 GB max RAM, 1.5 GB headroom, 50 GiB disk), restored the pinned bounded collection runner/schema/goldens, regenerated the notebook, and passed `259` public tests plus notebook JSON/AST validation.

# 2026-10-01: prefault static history RAM before search (A13)

StaticHybrid RAM arena now performs volatile startup stores at4KiB stride plus
final byte after malloc, before any search work. Includes size_t narrowing gate.
Avoids a merely lazy calloc/malloc reservation; startup OOM remains possible and
must be handled by orchestration. No memory locking/future residency guarantee,
search semantics or beam capacity change. Physical disk posix_fallocate remains.

## 2026-10-10 — exact maximum startup CUDA Graph pool
- Full 55,050,240-state 2×3060 acceptance exposed CUDA Graph instantiate OOM with 5,974 physical Stream3 rings; admission had fitted static bytes but not graph executables.

- Added explicit BEAM_RING_COUNT_LIMIT to derived automatic ring pools, validated positive uint32 before allocation. This bounds physical buffers/graph instances without changing beam, semantic shard capacity, candidate identity, or five-stream architecture. Default unchanged pending measured policy acceptance.
- Component cache now requires fresh physical RING_COUNT equality; stale old receipts without the field miss safely. Added regression tests for cap validation/frontier preservation/cache geometry.

- Native eager ensemble default physical ring limit is16 (explicit BEAM_RING_COUNT_LIMIT override); native-graph executor default preserved. This is a bounded scheduling baseline, not a claim that16 is globally optimal. GPU automatic acceptance still pending.

## 2026-10-10 — downstream selection with frozen expensive inference
- GPU8 1M A/B: full baseline9.64022s; near-plateau2048 fresh geometry9.36155s; staging-only2 with unchanged NN8192/J262144/shards8/sort4 gave9.53467s. Independent staging alone did not beat the2% material-gain gate; no cause claim about cache from these timings.
- Source criterion included constant Stream1 in its5% gain denominator. Heavy real NN could therefore hide meaningful downstream improvements. Component selector now uses summed downstream service work (Stream3/Stream4/transport/union), keeps frozen inference, records total service separately, remains an explicitly unverified wall-time proxy.
- Added independent staging-only proposal while preserving sort flush; bounded at six alternative exact-capacity candidates. Component cache policy/schema2 invalidates prior-policy receipts.
- Regression compares identical downstream probes under cheap and extremely expensive fixed inference; winner must not change merely because the constant inference term grows. CPU/GPU verification status follows in test_results.

Logical frontier, owner capacity, and sort geometry are preserved. This is only a lower-bound shortlist; native all-rank admission remains mandatory, including for older prepared runtimes. Component-cache schema bumped to 3 to invalidate receipts selected using the unbounded-ring pruning policy.
